Experiments on synthetic data and a real-world solar power generation forecasting task show that OVBAL is particularly effective under seller-centric pricing and yields a more favorable long-run error-cost trade-off in the real-world task under both pricing schemes.

</details>


### [112] [No-Free-Fairness: Fundamental Limits and Trade-offs in Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17810)
*Khoat Than*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"无免费公平"定理，证明学习系统中的不公平性源于三个固有因素：任务本身的成本结构、有限样本学习、以及模型表达能力的局限，而非仅仅是数据偏差或优化不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示学习系统中不公平性的根本来源，证明不公平并非仅由数据偏差或优化不足导致，而是内在于决策问题的结构、有限数据和模型表达能力之中的固有现象。

Method: 通过理论推导建立一组不可能性定理（No-Free-Fairness定理），分别从任务成本结构、有限样本学习、模型类表达能力三个维度分析公平性与性能之间的内在权衡关系。

Result: 证明了三个核心结果：(1) 任务存在子群不可约成本时，任何决策规则都必须在整体性能与差异之间权衡；(2) 即使存在理想公平解，有限样本学习也会引入子群差异，且严格相对公平会造成统计瓶颈，需指数级样本量；(3) 模型类本身的局限性可独立导致不公平。

Conclusion: 不公平性是决策问题内在结构、有限数据和模型表达能力共同作用的结果，实现公平需要明确的权衡，应将其作为核心设计考量。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a set of theoretical impossibility results, termed the No-Free-Fairness theorems, that identify three fundamental sources of disparity in learning systems. First, we show that when a task exhibits irreducible cost on a subgroup, any decision rule must trade off overall performance with disparity, yielding an inherent fairness--cost frontier. Second, we prove that even in ideal, noise-free settings where a perfectly fair and accurate solution exists, finite-sample learning alone induces nontrivial subgroup disparity, ruling out distribution-free fairness guarantees. More seriously, enforcing strict relative fairness creates a statistical bottleneck: achieving low cost may require exponentially many samples. Third, we show that limitations of the model class can independently induce disparity: if the model cannot represent accurate solutions for a subgroup, fairness remains unattainable regardless of data or training procedure. Overall, these results demonstrate that unfairness is not solely a consequence of biased data or suboptimal optimization, but arises from the intrinsic structure of decision problems, the constraints of finite data, and the expressivity of models. Our framework applies broadly beyond standard supervised learning, and suggests that achieving fairness requires explicit trade-offs and should be treated as a core design consideration.

</details>


### [113] [Conservation Laws for Modern Neural Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17816)
*Viet-Hoang Tran,Vinh Khanh Bui,Tan Lai Ngoc,Nam Nguyen,Tuan Dam,Tan M. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，用于刻画现代深度学习架构（包括GELU/SiLU/SwiGLU激活的前馈网络、多头注意力机制（含正弦/旋转位置编码）以及混合专家模型）中的梯度流守恒律，并通过实验验证了理论预测的不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降的隐式偏差（通过梯度流中的守恒律体现）是解释过参数化模型成功的关键，但现有研究主要集中在线性网络和ReLU网络，对现代架构（如GELU/SiLU/SwiGLU激活、注意力机制、混合专家模型）的守恒律尚不明确。

Method: 提出了一个统一的框架，用于刻画多种现代架构中的守恒律，包括：含GELU/SiLU/SwiGLU激活的前馈网络、含正弦/旋转位置编码的多头注意力机制、以及多种门控设计的混合专家模型。

Result: 理论推导出上述现代架构中的梯度流守恒律，并通过实验验证了所预测不变量的存在。

Conclusion: 建立的统一框架将守恒律分析从简单架构推广到现代深度学习模型，为理解过参数化模型的隐式偏差提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Understanding gradient descent dynamics is key to explaining the success of over-parameterized models, where implicit bias manifests through conservation laws in gradient flow. While such laws are well understood for linear and ReLU networks, they remain largely unexplored for modern architectures. This work develops a unified framework to characterize conservation laws for contemporary models, including feedforward networks with GELU, SiLU, and SwiGLU activations, multihead attention with sinusoidal and rotary positional encodings, and Mixture-of-Experts architectures under diverse gating designs. Our theoretical findings are supported by experiments that validate the predicted invariants.

</details>


### [114] [Functional Equivalence in Attention: A Comprehensive Study with Applications to Linear Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17830)
*Viet-Hoang Tran,Vinh Khanh Bui,Van-Hoan Trinh,Tan Lai Ngoc,Tan M. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer中位置编码对功能等价性的影响，发现正弦编码保持原始注意力结构的对称性，而旋转位置编码（RoPE）显著减少了对称群，增强了表达力，并影响线性模式连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对多头注意力的分析主要关注原始公式，忽略了位置编码对架构对称性的根本性重塑，需要系统研究位置编码如何影响Transformer的功能等价性。

Method: 对正弦位置编码和旋转位置编码（RoPE）两种最广泛使用的变体进行形式化分析，比较它们对Transformer功能等价结构的影响，并通过对齐算法实验验证线性模式连通性。

Result: 正弦编码保留了原始注意力机制的等价结构，而旋转编码显著减少了对称群，从而增强了表达力；位置编码对线性模式连通性的存在性和变异性起关键作用。

Conclusion: 该研究为RoPE在实践中日益突出的地位提供了原则性解释，并揭示了位置编码是影响Transformer功能等价性和连通性的关键因素。

Abstract: Neural network parameter spaces are inherently non-injective, as distinct parameter configurations can realize identical functions through functional equivalence. While this symmetry is well understood in classical fully connected and convolutional models, it becomes substantially more intricate in modern attention-based architectures. Existing analyses of multihead attention have largely focused on the vanilla formulation, overlooking positional encodings that fundamentally reshape architectural symmetries. In this work, we provide a formal study of functional equivalence in Transformers with positional encodings. Focusing on the two most widely used variants--sinusoidal and rotary positional encodings (RoPE)--we show that sinusoidal encodings preserve the equivalence structure of vanilla attention, whereas rotary encodings significantly reduce the symmetry group, thereby enhancing expressivity. This offers a principled explanation for the growing prominence of RoPE in practice. We further examine how positional encodings affect linear mode connectivity, and through an alignment algorithm, empirically demonstrate that the presence and variability of connectivity across Transformer settings crucially depend on the positional encoding.

</details>


### [115] [From Drift to Coherence: Stabilizing Beliefs in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17832)
*SongEun Kim,Seungyoo Lee,Edwin Fong,Hyungi Lee,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在多项选择问答中的信念动态，发现预测信念存在早期漂移（违反鞅性质），但经过多次自回归重采样后能自稳定并收敛到一致的预测分布。基于此，提出了种子答案提示策略（加速稳定）和自一致性损失（通过微调吸收早期漂移），在不牺牲准确性的前提下显著减少了信念漂移并提高了预测一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究在受控的上下文学习设置中发现LLM的预测信念不满足鞅性质（一致性条件），但尚不清楚在更典型的使用场景（如多项选择问答）中信念动态如何表现。

Method: 提出提示式预测重采样（PPR），通过自回归方式对同一问题生成多个答案，计算精确预测分布并研究信念动态；进一步提出种子答案提示策略和自一致性损失微调方法。

Result: PPR揭示了早期信念漂移（鞅性质违反），但多次重采样后信念自稳定并收敛；提出的两种方法在不牺牲准确性的情况下显著减少了信念漂移并提高了预测一致性。

Conclusion: LLM的预测信念虽然初期不满足鞅性质，但具有自稳定能力，通过提示策略和微调可以加速这一过程，实现更一致的预测。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often hypothesized to perform implicit Bayesian inference, yet a key coherence condition, the martingale property of predictive beliefs, has been shown to fail in controlled synthetic in-context learning settings. We revisit this question in a more typical usage regime: generic multiple-choice question answering. Exploiting the discrete answer space, we compute exact predictive distributions and study belief dynamics induced by autoregressive answer resampling. We introduce prompted predictive resampling (PPR), where an LLM generates a sequence of answers to the same question. Empirically, PPR reveals early-stage belief drift, indicating martingale violations. However, after sufficient resampling steps, the belief process self-stabilizes and converges to a coherent predictive distribution. Based on this observation, we further propose (i) a seed-answer prompting strategy to accelerate stabilization, and (ii) a self-consistency loss that amortizes early-stage drift into the model via fine-tuning. Experiments on multiple-choice QA benchmarks show that our methods substantially reduce belief drift and improve predictive coherence without sacrificing accuracy.

</details>


### [116] [Meta-classification of one-class classification models using ranking correlation and nearest neighbor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17858)
*Toshitaka Hayashi,Hamido Fujita,Dalibor Cimr,Richard Cimler,Jitka Kühnová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对一类分类（OCC）模型进行元分类的方法，将OCC模型表示为正常性排序，使用最近邻和排序相关性度量进行分类。实验表明该方法能高精度区分数据集、算法和超参数，本质上实现了对数据集的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将机器学习技术应用于ML模型本身是一个未被探索的方向，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将OCC模型表示为正常性排序（normality rankings），采用最近邻（nearest-neighbor）和排序相关性度量（ranking-correlation metrics）对其进行分类。

Result: 当类别标签对应数据集时达到高精度；当训练数据包含相同类别时能够区分算法；还能使用睡眠记录数据实现数据集的分类。

Conclusion: 分类OCC模型本质上等同于将多个样本视为单一输入来分类数据集。该方法为分类OCC模型、数据集和排序提供了统一解决方案。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) techniques have been applied to various problems. However, applying ML to ML models is an unexplored direction. For this purpose, this paper considers a meta-classification of one-class classification (OCC) models, because all ML models could be approximated as OCC models. The proposal represents OCC models as normality rankings and classifies them using nearest-neighbor and ranking-correlation metrics. The experiment classifies OCC models, where classes correspond to training datasets, algorithms, and hyperparameters. The proposal achieves high accuracy when class labels are datasets. Moreover, it can classify algorithms when the training datasets contain the same class. In addition, the discussion highlights that the classification of OCC models is essentially the classification of datasets that treats multiple samples as a single input. The experiment demonstrates the classification of datasets using sleeping records. The proposed method can provide a unified solution for classifying OCC models, datasets, and rankings. Source code is uploaded to the public repository https://github.com/ToshiHayashi/ClassOCC.

</details>


### [117] [AnchorKV: Safety-Aware KV Cache Compression via Soft Penalty with a Refusal Anchor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17872)
*Ning Ni,Yingjie Lao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnchorKV是一种对KV缓存压缩的改进方法，通过在键空间中构建安全锚点来偏向标记保留分数，使其远离与有害提示相关的方向，从而在不显著降低效用的前提下提升安全对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法在减少内存和能源开销方面有效，但它们的压缩策略要么无法防御越狱攻击，要么在激进驱逐时损害安全对齐。需要在保持压缩效率的同时增强模型安全性。

Method: 提出AnchorKV，采用离线方式构建安全锚点：基于表示工程中的均值差方法，将其适配到KV缓存使用的层特定键投影空间。然后基于该锚点设计软惩罚标记选择规则，在保留分数中偏向远离有害提示方向，通过牺牲少量效用换取安全性提升，当惩罚为零时退化为原始压缩器。

Result: 在保持原有压缩效率的同时，显著提升了模型对越狱攻击的防御能力，且仅以少量效用损失为代价。

Conclusion: AnchorKV作为一种即插即用的修改方法，能够有效平衡KV缓存压缩中的效用与安全对齐，为解决大语言模型部署中的安全与效率权衡提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) outperform earlier architectures on generative inference and long-context tasks, but their large size introduces significant challenges in memory usage, energy cost, and on-device deployment. Since scaling pre-trained language models improves downstream capability \cite{zhao2023survey}, the key-value (KV) cache becomes a dominant inference bottleneck. Recent KV cache compression methods \cite{jo2025fastkv,li2024snapkv,zhou2024dynamickv} reduce this cost by retaining only a subset of attention-relevant tokens. However, while these approaches preserve accuracy on benign workloads, their compression policies either fail to defend against jailbreak attacks \cite{jiang2024robustkv} or degrade safety alignment under aggressive eviction.
  We propose AnchorKV, a drop-in modification to KV cache compression that biases token retention scores away from directions in key space associated with harmful prompts. AnchorKV constructs an offline safety anchor by adapting a difference-of-means representation engineering approach \cite{arditi2024refusal,zou2023representation} to the layer-specific key projection space used in KV caching. Based on this anchor, a soft penalty token selection rule trades a small amount of utility for substantially improved safety alignment, while reducing to the original compressor when the penalty is zero.

</details>


### [118] [Monotonic Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks: A Theoretical and Empirical Study of Monotonicity as an Inductive Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17886)
*Mikhail Krasnov,Carolina Fortuna,Blaž Bertalanič*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MKAN，一种具有硬单调性保证的KAN变体，通过指数重参数化B样条系数、正边权值和单调基激活实现对所有参数值的单调性约束，并证明了表示代价定理：任何C^K特征提取器诱导的球状语义邻域划分可用至多2N^*个单调坐标等价实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单调神经网络方法（MLP或流模型）缺乏每边功能透明性；已有的单调KAN变体MonoKAN仅对部分参数施加约束且需投影式训练。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出一种在所有参数值上保证硬单调性且保持KAN透明性的方法。

Method: 通过指数重参数化B样条系数、使用正边权值和单调基激活函数，在KAN架构中实现硬单调性约束，使得训练退化为标准的无约束梯度下降。同时提出表示代价定理，证明单调编码器的理论规模上界为2N^*。

Result: 在SMM/ICML-2024基准上与SOTA单调NN竞争力相当，是唯一同时具有硬无约束单调性和KAN每边功能透明性的方法。2N^*预测在四个真实数据集的自监督特征大小扫描中得到验证，在受控单调生成数据集上MKAN比KAN、MLP和线性基线具有更高的Spearman对齐度。

Conclusion: MKAN成功将硬单调性保证与KAN的透明性结合，提供了架构无关的单调编码器规模设置原则，实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: Monotonicity has been a long-running architectural inductive bias for neural networks, motivated by tabular, scientific, and economic settings where outputs are known to respond monotonically to certain inputs. Existing approaches are MLP- or flow-based and lack per-edge functional transparency; the only Kolmogorov--Arnold Network (KAN) variant with monotonicity, MonoKAN, enforces the constraint only on a restricted parameter subset and requires a projection-style training procedure. We close this gap with \textbf{MKAN}, a KAN with hard monotonicity guaranteed for \emph{all} parameter values via exponential reparameterization of B-spline coefficients, positive edge weights, and a monotone base activation. Training reduces to standard unconstrained gradient descent. Our headline theoretical contribution is a \emph{representation-cost} theorem: any $C^K, K >0$ feature extractor inducing a ball-shaped semantic-neighborhood partition admits a monotone realization of the equivalent neighborhood structure at $N' = N^* + k \le 2N^*$, where $k$ is the number of non-monotone coordinates of the original. The bound is architecture-agnostic and gives a principled sizing rule for monotone encoders. Empirically, MKAN is competitive with state-of-the-art monotone NNs on the SMM/ICML-2024 benchmark while being the only method that combines hard unconstrained monotonicity with KAN's per-edge functional transparency; the $2N^*$ prediction is validated in a self-supervised feature-size sweep on four real datasets, and on a controlled monotone-generative dataset MKAN recovers ground-truth factors with substantially higher Spearman alignment than KAN, MLP, and linear baselines.

</details>


### [119] [Dimensionality Controls When Modularity Helps in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17889)
*Kathrin Korte,Christian Medeiros Adriano,Joachim Winther Pedersen,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了模组化架构、任务相似性和表征维度如何共同影响组合持续学习。在高维"惰性"状态下，显式模组化结构影响不大；在低维"丰富"状态下，模组化网络能发展出分级任务特异性子空间，形成更具组合性和可解释性的组织方式。初始缩放诱导的表征状态是决定模组化结构在持续学习中是否受益的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解组合学习系统中可塑性（获取新知识）与稳定性（保留已学组件）之间的平衡机制，特别是当任务共享结构并存在干扰风险时，模组化架构、任务相似性和表征维度如何共同影响持续学习性能。

Method: 采用顺序A-B-A范式，比较任务分区循环网络与单网络基线，通过权重尺度操作诱导高维和低维表征状态，分析不同条件下网络内部几何结构和性能差异。

Result: 高维"惰性"状态下两种架构性能相似；低维"丰富"状态下模组化网络表现优异，能形成分级任务特异性子空间：相似任务子空间重叠，中等不相似任务部分对齐，不相似任务分离。

Conclusion: 初始化尺度诱导的表征状态（与表征维度共变）是决定模组化结构在持续学习中是否功能受益的关键因素。安全性和鲁棒性应被视为表征子空间的自适应分配问题，而非固定分离或共享。

Abstract: Compositional learning systems must balance plasticity, the ability to acquire new knowledge, with stability, the preservation of previously learned components, especially when tasks share structure and risk interference. We study how modular architecture, task similarity, and representational dimensionality jointly shape compositional continual learning in a sequential A-B-A paradigm, comparing a task-partitioned recurrent network to a single-network baseline while inducing high- and low-dimensional regimes via weight-scale manipulations. In a high-dimensional "lazy" regime, both architectures achieve similar performance and internal geometry, suggesting that explicit modular structure has little impact when representations are weakly constrained. In a lower-dimensional "rich" regime, modularity becomes decisive: the modular network develops graded task-specific subspaces that overlap for similar tasks, partially align for moderately dissimilar tasks, and separate for dissimilar tasks, yielding a more compositional and interpretable organization than the single network. These findings identify the representational regime induced by initialization scale, which co-varies with representational dimensionality, as a key factor governing when compositional, modular structure is functionally beneficial in continual learning, and support viewing safety and robustness as problems of adaptive allocation of representational subspaces rather than fixed separation versus sharing.

</details>


### [120] [KANLib -- An Modular, Extensible and Fast Kolmogorov-Arnold Network Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17927)
*Julian Hoever,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KANLib，一个模块化、可扩展且计算高效的KAN架构开发与评估框架，统一了现有KAN实现（PyKAN、EfficientKAN、FastKAN），支持多种基函数类型、自适应网格缩放和细粒度架构定制，在加州房价基准测试中复现了参考实现的预测行为并实现了有竞争力的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KANs虽有理论优势，但实际研究因高计算成本和现有框架功能不一致而困难重重，亟需一个统一、高效、可扩展的框架来推动KAN的实践研究。

Method: KANLib采用模块化、可扩展的软件架构，统一了PyKAN、EfficientKAN、FastKAN等实现的核心概念，支持两种基函数类型、自适应网格缩放、网格扩展和细粒度架构定制，并与标准PyTorch工作流兼容。

Result: 在加州房价基准测试中，KANLib复现了参考KAN实现的预测行为，实现了有竞争力的计算效率，并能以微小性能代价探索标准KAN之外的架构变体。

Conclusion: KANLib为未来可扩展和可扩展的KAN架构研究提供了稳健的基础。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to traditional multilayer perceptrons by replacing linear weights with learnable univariate functions. Despite their theoretical advantages in interpretability and expressiveness, practical research of KANs remains difficult due to high computational costs and inconsistent feature support across existing frameworks. This paper introduces KANLib, a modular, extensible, and computationally efficient framework for developing and evaluating KAN architectures. KANLib unifies core concepts from existing implementations, including PyKAN, EfficientKAN, and FastKAN, within a consistent software architecture that emphasizes flexibility, feature parity, and high performance. The framework supports two basis function types, adaptive grid rescaling, grid extension, and fine-grained architectural customization while maintaining compatibility with standard PyTorch workflows. Experimental evaluation on the California Housing benchmark demonstrates that KANLib reproduces the predictive behavior of established reference KAN implementations while achieving competitive computational efficiency. Furthermore, the framework enables the exploration of architectural variations beyond standard KAN formulations with only minor impacts on predictive performance. Overall, KANLib provides a robust foundation for future research on scalable and extensible KAN architectures.

</details>


### [121] [Predictive Analytics in E-Commerce for CustomerBehavior Forecasting using hybrid Ret-DNN withXGBoost Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17931)
*Degala Pushpa Sri,Mayank Atreya,Lakshmi. H,Navin Chhibber,Mukesh Soni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合Ret-DNN与XGBoost的模型，用于根据英国在线零售商的近50万条交易记录预测客户购买概率，最终MAE达到0.2193，优于纯Ret-DNN模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台难以理解客户行为并预测其未来购买，亟需有效方法来克服该挑战。

Method: 先对UK零售数据进行清洗、异常处理、时序特征提取、编码及Z-score归一化等预处理；再通过Ret-DNN提取完整交易上下文特征；最后将特征输入XGBoost模型预测客户购买概率。

Result: 所提出的Ret-DNN+XGBoost混合模型取得了MAE 0.2193，优于现有的纯Ret-DNN模型。

Conclusion: 混合Ret-DNN与XGBoost的方法能够有效捕捉零售数据的时序特征和表格动态，提升购买行为预测性能。

Abstract: In recent years, electronic (E) commerce services have rapidly increased in the daily lives of people, which helpsthem to purchase products online. However, retail platforms have struggled to understand customer behavior and make it difficult to predict their future purchases. To overcome these challenges, this study proposes a hybrid Retail Deep NeuralNetwork (Ret-DNN) with an Extreme Gradient Boosting(XGBoost) model for capturing temporal features and tabular dynamics of retail data. First, data were sourced from a UnitedKingdom (UK)-based online retailer that contains transactions with almost 500,000 records. Then, the collected data were pre-processed using a series of techniques, such as data cleaning, outlier handling, temporal feature extraction, feature encoding, and z-score normalization, to ensure that the data were ready for model training and testing. Subsequently, the preprocessed data were fed into the Ret-DNN model, which acts as a feature extractor to understand the complete context of customer transactions. Further, the extracted data were fed as input into the XGBoost model, which predicted the final output as the purchase probability of customers. Finally, the proposed Ret-DNN XGBoost model achieved better results by attaining aMean Absolute Error (MAE) 0.2193 when compared to the existing Ret-DNN model.
  Keywords: Customer behavior forecasting, extreme gradientboosting, electronic commerce, predictive analytic, retail deepneural networks.

</details>


### [122] [SoftMoE: Soft Differentiable Routing for Mixture-of-Experts in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17952)
*Mikołaj Zasada,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor,Marcin Kurdziel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftMoE提出用可微的软top-k路由替代传统MoE中的离散top-k路由，并引入可学习的每层专家激活数及全局预算约束，在激活更少专家的情况下达到或超越传统稀疏MoE的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏MoE使用离散的top-k路由不可微，导致每输入激活固定数量的专家，计算效率低下，且无法通过梯度优化路由分配。

Method: 1) 用截断的soft top-k LapSum松弛替代离散top-k路由，实现可微路由；2) 将每层平均激活专家数参数化，施加全局预算约束，让模型学习跨层分配专家容量。

Result: 在语言建模和下游任务上达到或优于稀疏MoE的性能，同时激活显著更少的专家；学习到的专家分配高度非均匀，深层网络激活更多专家。

Conclusion: SoftMoE在保持与自回归建模完全兼容的同时，实现了更高效的专家路由和计算分配。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable scaling LLM parameters under a fixed inference budget by activating only a small subset of experts via top-$k$ routing. While this preserves causality and suits autoregressive language models, the discrete top-$k$ operator is not differentiable, forcing a fixed number of active experts per input and resulting in inefficient use of computation. We propose SoftMoE, which replaces discrete routing with a truncated soft top-$k$ LapSum relaxation, allowing gradient-based optimization of expert routing. We further parameterize the mean number of active experts per layer and impose a global budget constraint, enabling the model to learn how to allocate expert capacity across layers. SoftMoE remains fully compatible with autoregressive modeling and achieves performance comparable to or better than sparse MoE on language modeling and downstream tasks, while activating significantly fewer experts. Notably, the learned allocation is highly non-uniform, with later layers activating more experts. The source code is publicly available$^\dagger$.

</details>


### [123] [Multiple cyclicity and Wavelet Decomposition with Channel Correlation for Long-term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17996)
*Bin Wang,Heming Yang,Jinfang Sheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出McWC模型，通过分别建模周期、趋势和通道间相关性，并利用多层周期构建、多层感知机、多级小波分解和频域损失函数，在六个真实数据集上实现了长时序列预测的最新性能，同时保持了高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时序列预测工作忽略了真实世界中时间序列数据的通道间相关性，且依赖复杂设计导致计算效率低下。

Method: McWC包含四个部分：1)多层周期构建模块解耦周期信息；2)多层感知机提取通道间相关性；3)多级小波分解模块建模并融合多层高低频信息；4)聚合各分量结果得到输出。同时通过频域损失函数解耦通道内自相关性。

Result: 在六个真实数据集上达到最先进性能，展现出优异的计算效率和历史信息提取能力。

Conclusion: McWC通过分别建模周期、趋势和通道间相关性，有效解决了现有方法忽略通道间相关性和计算效率低的问题，实现了长时序列预测的性能提升。

Abstract: Cyclicity and trend are important components of time series data and many studies based on cyclicity and trend have achieved good results in long-term time series forecasting. However, we believe that current work neglects the influence of real-world inter-channel correlations in time series data which leads to suboptimal predictions. Furthermore, these models rely on complex designs to capture diverse information so that resulting in low computational efficiency. To address this challenge, we propose McWC, a long-term time series forecasting model that separately models the cyclicity, trend, and inter-channel correlations. Specifically, McWC first decouples cyclical information from data using a multi-layer cyclicity construction module. Then, it extracts inter-channel correlations using multi-layer perceptron. Next, it models and fuses the multi-layer high-frequency and low-frequency information from data using a multi-level wavelet decomposition module. Finally, it aggregates the results of different components to obtain the output. Simultaneously, we decouple intra-channel autocorrelations by calculating a loss function in the frequency domain. Experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that McWC achieves state-of-the-art performance, exhibiting excellent computational efficiency and historical information extraction capabilities.

</details>


### [124] [Half a Link can Be Enough to Predict a Whole Link: Understanding Generalization in Knowledge Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18001)
*Cosimo Gregucci,Obaidah Theeb,Daniel Hernandez,Antonio Vergari,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究知识图谱基础模型（KGFMs）的零样本泛化机制，提出"半链接"（half-links）概念，发现KGFMs在未见知识图谱上的性能取决于测试三元组中半链接(h,r)或(r,t)是否在推理图中出现。通过四类场景的严格分层分析，揭示当前最优模型利用已见半链接进行预测，而未见过半链接则带来不同挑战。该细粒度分类可作为诊断KGFM鲁棒泛化的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解KGFMs在未见知识图谱上进行零样本泛化时的鲁棒性机制尚不清楚，需要揭示其泛化能力的边界和失效场景。

Method: 提出"半链接"概念，将测试三元组(h,r,t)拆解为(h,r)和(r,t)两部分，根据这些半链接在推理图中是否出现构建四类场景的分类法，并在该分类体系下对当前最优KGFMs进行严格分层分析。

Result: SoTA KGFMs依赖已见的半链接进行预测，当半链接未见过时面临不同挑战；模型在不同场景下的性能表现不均匀。

Conclusion: 该细粒度分类可作为诊断KGFM鲁棒泛化的协议，并为改进新型KGFMs提供方向。

Abstract: Knowledge graph (KG) foundation models (KGFMs) are zero-shot generalizers: trained once, they can predict links on unseen graphs without retraining. However, understanding when and how they can robustly generalize across KGs is still an open question. In this paper, we shed some light on their generalization mechanisms highlighting how their performance on unseen KGs is not uniform when it comes to partially seen links, which we call half-links. In fact, we show that to predict a test triple $(h,r,t)$ it might suffice in practice to have observed the half-link $(h,r)$ or $(r,t)$ in the inference graph. This yields a taxonomy of four scenarios when combinations of these half-links are observed or not. In a rigorous stratified analysis over these scenarios, we reveal that SoTA KGFMs use seen half links for predictions, while unseen half-links pose different challenges. As such, our finer-grained taxonomy can be a diagnostic protocol for robust KGFM generalization and highlights where novel KGFMs can improve.

</details>


### [125] [C2FL: Clustered Continual Federated Learning under Spatial and Temporal Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18003)
*Davide Domini,Gianluca Aguzzi,Lorenzo Pellegrini,Mirko Viroli,Lukas Esterle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出C2FL，一种完全分布式的联邦学习方法，通过空间聚类自组织学习群组，并结合经验回放与驻留时间感知的自适应平均来应对时空漂移，从而在保护隐私的同时实现集体自适应系统的鲁棒学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体自适应系统（CAS）中节点感知数据常涉及隐私敏感信息，无法集中收集；节点移动导致空间异质性（相近节点感知相似、远处节点差异大）和时间漂移（模型逐渐过时），这些因素严重削弱传统学习策略，亟需一种兼顾隐私、空间异质性和时间漂移的去中心化学习方法。

Method: C2FL包含两个核心机制：(1) 节点通过空间聚类自组织形成学习群组，反映环境的地理结构；(2) 每个节点结合经验回放与驻留时间感知的自适应平均步骤——在相同区域停留越久，逐渐融入区域共识，同时在分布变化时保留先前学到的知识。

Result: 在系统重现空间和时间偏移的合成实验中，标准联邦学习策略在这些条件下性能显著下降，而C2FL恢复了鲁棒的集体自适应能力。

Conclusion: C2FL能够有效应对隐私约束、空间异质性和时间漂移三者交织的挑战，为移动CAS场景提供了一种可行的分布式学习方案。

Abstract: Collective Adaptive Systems (CAS) increasingly rely on machine learning to let each node learn from locally sensed data, aligning its behavior with the surrounding environment. Scaling this intelligence, however, raises fundamental challenges: sensed data is often privacy-sensitive, preventing centralized collection; nodes are mobile, traversing regions where nearby nodes perceive similar phenomena while distant ones observe radically different conditions, creating natural spatial clusters; and these distributions evolve over time due to mobility, introducing temporal drift that makes local models progressively stale. These dynamics arise across domains - vehicular sensing, drone-based monitoring, smartphone crowdsensing - yet the interplay of privacy, spatial heterogeneity, and temporal drift severely undermines conventional learning strategies. Therefore, we propose C2FL, a fully distributed Federated Learning (FL) approach where nodes self-organize into learning groups through spatial clustering, reflecting the geographic structure of the environment. To counteract temporal drift, each node combines experience replay with a dwell-time-aware adaptive averaging step, progressively incorporating the regional consensus as it remains longer within the same area, while preserving previously acquired knowledge under evolving distributions. We evaluate our approach on synthetic experiments that systematically reproduce spatial and temporal shifts, showing that standard federated strategies degrade significantly under these conditions and that our method restores robust collective adaptation.

</details>


### [126] [Recursive Scaling in Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18022)
*Alba Carballo-Castro,Julianna Piskorz,Paulius Rauba,Mihaela van der Schaar,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归掩码扩散模型（R-MDMs），通过在扩散步骤中重复应用相同的去噪Transformer来增加递归深度作为第三缩放轴，在不增加参数量的情况下提升有效模型深度，在Sudoku和Countdown等结构化生成任务中，L次递归迭代能达到非递归基线模型约L倍参数的性能，并能部分替代额外去噪步骤，实现参数效率和测试时计算分配的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MDMs的扩展仅通过增加参数量或去噪步数实现，缺乏其他有效的缩放维度。作者希望探索递归深度作为第三缩放轴的可行性，在不增加参数的前提下提升模型容量和生成质量。

Method: R-MDMs在每个扩散步骤内重复应用相同的去噪Transformer（递归），通过参数复用在单步内实现迭代式输出精炼，从而增加有效模型深度而参数总量不变。该方法在推理时可通过调整递归次数灵活分配计算资源。

Result: 在Sudoku和Countdown等结构化生成任务中，L次递归迭代的R-MDM模型性能与约L倍参数量的非递归基线模型相当；递归精炼可部分替代额外去噪步数，用更少的前向传播达到相同生成质量。

Conclusion: 递归深度是MDMs的一种实用缩放机制，能同时提升参数效率和测试时计算资源的分配灵活性。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for sequence generation. Scaling MDMs is conventionally achieved by increasing the parameter count or the number of denoising steps. We introduce Recursive Masked Diffusion Models (R-MDMs), which add recursive depth as a third scaling axis by repeatedly applying the same denoising transformer within each diffusion step. Recursion enables iterative refinement of the output through parameter reuse, increasing effective model depth without increasing parameter count. Across structured generation tasks, including Sudoku and Countdown, we show that R-MDMs achieve substantially improved parameter efficiency: a model with $L$ recursive iterations often matches the performance of non-recursive baselines with roughly $L\times$ more parameters. Moreover, recursive refinement can partially substitute for additional denoising steps, allowing recursive models to reach the same generation quality with fewer forward passes at inference time. These results suggest that recursive depth is a practically useful scaling mechanism for MDMs, improving both parameter efficiency and the allocation of test-time compute.

</details>


### [127] [LoopCoder-v2: Only Loop Once for Efficient Test-Time Computation Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18023)
*Jian Yang,Shawn Guo,Wei Zhang,Tianyu Zheng,Yaxin Du,Haau-Sing Li,Jiajun Wu,Yue Song,Yan Xing,Qingsong Cai,Zelong Huang,Chuan Hao,Ran Tao,Xianglong Liu,Wayne Xin Zhao,Mingjie Tang,Weifeng Lv,Ming Zhou,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并行循环Transformer（PLT），通过跨循环位置偏移（CLP）和共享KV门控滑动窗口注意力解决循环Transformer的延迟和KV缓存问题。基于增益-成本视角研究循环次数选择，训练了7B参数的不同循环次数PLT代码模型LoopCoder-v2。实验表明两循环变体在代码生成、推理、Agent软件工程等任务上显著优于无循环基线（SWE-bench从43.0提升至64.4），但三循环及以上出现性能回退。诊断发现第二循环提供主要精炼增益，后续循环产生递减振荡更新，而CLP位置偏移成本相对固定，导致增益-成本失衡，解释了PLT在两次循环时饱和的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过重复共享块扩展潜在计算，但顺序循环增加了延迟和KV缓存内存开销。PLT通过CLP和共享KV门控滑动窗口注意力缓解了这些成本，使循环次数成为实用的设计选择。因此需要研究如何选择最优循环次数，从增益-成本视角理解循环次数对模型性能的影响。

Method: 提出并行循环Transformer（PLT）架构，包含跨循环位置偏移（CLP）和共享KV门控滑动窗口注意力机制。从零开始训练7B参数的不同循环次数（0/1/2/3/4）代码模型LoopCoder-v2，共用18T tokens，后进行配对的指令微调。基于增益-成本视角分析循环次数选择：额外循环可精炼表示，但CLP在每个循环边界引入位置不匹配。

Result: 两循环变体在代码生成、代码推理、Agent软件工程和工具使用基准测试中全面优于无循环基线，SWE-bench Verified从43.0提升至64.4，Multi-SWE从14.0提升至31.0。三循环及以上变体性能回退，呈现强非单调循环次数效应。诊断显示循环2提供主要精炼增益，后续循环产生递减、振荡更新和降低的表示多样性。CLP导致的位置不匹配成本相对固定，随着精炼增益缩小，偏移成本日益主导。

Conclusion: 增益-成本权衡解释了PLT在两次循环时饱和的现象，为循环次数选择提供了诊断工具。两循环是最优选择，后续循环的递减收益与固定的位置偏移成本失衡导致性能回退。

Abstract: Looped Transformers scale latent computation by repeatedly applying shared blocks, but sequential looping increases latency and KV-cache memory with the loop count. Parallel loop Transformers (PLT) alleviate this cost through cross-loop position offsets (CLP) and shared-KV gated sliding-window attention, making loop count a practical design choice. We therefore study PLT loop-count selection through a gain--cost view: an extra loop may refine representations, but CLP also introduces a positional mismatch at each loop boundary. We instantiate this study by training LoopCoder-v2, a family of 7B PLT coders with different loop counts, from scratch on 18T tokens, followed by matched instruction tuning and evaluation. Empirically, the two-loop variant delivers broad gains over the non-looped baseline across code generation, code reasoning, agentic software engineering, and tool-use benchmarks, improving SWE-bench Verified from 43.0 to 64.4 points and Multi-SWE from 14.0 to 31.0 points. In contrast, variants with three or more loops regress, revealing a strongly non-monotonic loop-count effect. Our diagnostics show that loop 2 provides the main productive refinement, while later loops yield diminishing, oscillatory updates and reduced representational diversity. Because the CLP-induced mismatch remains roughly fixed as refinement gains shrink, the offset cost increasingly dominates. This gain--cost trade-off explains PLT's saturation at two loops and provides diagnostics for loop-count selection.

</details>


### [128] [Catastrophic Forgetting is Low-Rank: A Function-Space Theory for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18024)
*Ido Nitzan Hidekel,Dan Raviv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在神经正切核（NTK）框架下给出了持续学习中灾难性遗忘的函数空间解释，推导出新任务训练引起旧任务预测漂移的闭式表达式，发现遗忘集中在少数旧任务NTK本征模式中，并据此提出一种针对性的谱正则化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续适应中灾难性遗忘的研究通常从参数漂移、重放或蒸馏角度出发，未能识别哪些输出空间方向是脆弱的。本文旨在给出一个函数空间的解释，从NTK框架分析遗忘机制。

Method: 在NTK机制下推导出新任务训练前后旧任务预测漂移的闭式解，该解由跨任务核决定；对于冻结主干线性头的PEFT-CL模型（参数线性），该预测器精确成立；对于非线性适配器或全微调，作为局部NTK近似。进一步揭示遗忘集中于少数旧任务NTK本征模式，并在冻结线性头下给出脆弱秩的Kronecker缩放规则。

Result: 推导出遗忘向量的闭式预测器；发现遗忘集中在少量本征模式；建立Kronecker缩放规则解释脆弱秩；揭示参数空间正则化器无法捕捉输出空间干扰的原因；提出针对性谱正则化方法。

Conclusion: NTK视角下的函数空间分析能更精确地识别持续学习中遗忘的关键方向，谱正则化是比参数空间正则化更有效的干预手段。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting in continual adaptation is usually studied through parameter drift, replay, or distillation, but these views do not identify which output-space directions are vulnerable. We give a function-space account in the NTK regime: new-task training induces old-task prediction drift through the cross-task kernel, yielding a closed-form predictor for the forgetting vector before any new-task gradient step. In frozen-backbone linear-head PEFT-CL, where the model is linear in the trainable parameters, the predictor is exact up to numerical precision; for nonlinear adapters/full fine-tuning, it is a local NTK approximation. The same expression reveals that forgetting concentrates in a small number of old-task NTK eigenmodes and under frozen linear heads gives a Kronecker scaling rule for the vulnerable rank. These results clarify the relation to prior NTK-overlap theory, explain why parameter-space regularizers can miss output-space interference, and motivate a targeted spectral regularizer.

</details>


### [129] [ConTex: Reformulating Counterfactual Generation For Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18049)
*Jan Voets,Hasan Tercan,Tobias Meisen,Sebastian Baum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConTex提出了一种基于全局一致干预策略的反事实生成方法，用于深度学习时间序列预测，通过解耦架构实现单次前向传播生成可解释的稀疏反事实，显著降低计算成本（12-36倍）并支持实时推理（约0.007秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测的反事实解释方法依赖实例级优化，导致跨实例不一致、计算成本高，难以应用于实时场景。需要一种全局一致的干预策略来克服这些局限。

Method: 提出ConTex，一种模型无关的解耦架构，包含时序上下文编码器和条件编码器，以及两个分别捕捉时间相关性和干预强度的输出头。通过学习单一共享函数生成全局一致的干预策略，单次前向传播即可产生反事实解释。

Result: 在多个预测架构和基准数据集上达到最优有效性；生成稀疏反事实，最小化必要干预数量；计算成本降低12-36倍；支持实时推理（约0.007秒）。

Conclusion: ConTex通过全局一致的干预策略有效解决了实例级反事实生成的不一致和高计算成本问题，为时间序列预测提供了一种高效、可解释的实时反事实解释方法。

Abstract: Decision-making with deep learning-based time series forecasting requires not only accurate predictions but also actionable insights. However, current architectures do not inherently provide such information. Specifically, guidance is needed on how current conditions must be modified to shift from a predicted outcome to a desired future scenario. Counterfactual explanations provide a natural framework for this task, as they represent minimal input changes that alter the model's prediction, indicating when and how intervention is required. Existing approaches rely on instance-wise optimization, leading to inconsistency across instances, high computational costs, and limited applicability in real-time settings.
  To address these limitations, we reformulate counterfactual generation for time series forecasting as the problem of learning a globally consistent intervention strategy, allowing counterfactuals to be generated through a single shared function. We propose Counterfactual Time Series Explanations (ConTex), a model-agnostic, decomposed architecture comprising a temporal context encoder and a conditional encoder, followed by two heads that capture interventions in terms of temporal relevance and modification strength. This structure overcomes the instability and inconsistency of instance-based approaches by producing targeted, interpretable interventions across time and feature dimensions in a single forward pass, making it suitable for real-time applications.
  Across multiple forecasting architectures and benchmark datasets, ConTex achieves state-of-the-art validity while generating sparse counterfactuals that minimize the number of necessary interventions. Additionally, our approach reduces computational cost by at least 12-36x compared to instance-wise generation and supports real-time inference at approximately 0.007 seconds.

</details>


### [130] [NoiseTilt: Noise-Tilted Reverse Kernels for Diffusion Reward Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18066)
*Jisung Hwang,Yunhong Min,Jaihoon Kim,I-Chao Shen,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出噪声倾斜逆核（NTRK），一种奖励引导的扩散采样方法，通过将奖励梯度注入噪声项而非改变逆向均值，在保持预训练模型不变的同时实现单步单样本的高效引导，在多个任务上以20倍计算量减少超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励引导扩散采样方法存在两难困境：基于梯度的方法通过改变逆向均值引导生成，但会将中间状态推出模型训练区域，降低生成质量；基于搜索的方法能保持质量，但无法利用梯度信号。现有方法无法同时实现高质量与高效引导。

Method: NTRK保持逆向均值固定，将奖励梯度注入噪声项来偏向高奖励区域。核心机制是引入白化算子，使奖励梯度能够安全地作为噪声注入而不损失引导信号，从而避免扰动预训练模型习得的分布。

Result: 在多个奖励对齐任务中，NTRK在不损失样本质量的情况下优于最新基线。在美学生成任务上，NTRK仅用25次NFE就超越了最佳基线在500次NFE时的奖励得分，实现了20倍的计算量减少。

Conclusion: NTRK通过噪声项注入奖励梯度而非改变逆向均值，并借助白化算子解决了梯度引导与生成质量之间的根本性权衡，为奖励引导扩散采样提供了高效且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce the Noise-Tilted Reverse Kernel (NTRK), a reward-guided diffusion sampler that injects reward gradients through the noise term, leaving the pretrained reverse kernel unchanged and requiring only a single sample per step. Reward-guided sampling at inference time has greatly expanded the versatility of pretrained diffusion models. Yet existing methods face a trade-off. Gradient-based guidance shifts the reverse mean, steering generation but pushing intermediate states outside the region that the model was trained on and degrading quality. Search-based methods preserve quality but gain no gradient signal. No prior method achieves both. NTRK resolves this by keeping the reverse mean fixed and biasing the noise term toward high reward. We introduce a whitening operator, the central mechanism behind NTRK, that makes the reward gradient safe to inject as noise without losing its guiding signal. Across various reward alignment tasks, NTRK outperforms recent state-of-the-art baselines without losing sample quality. Remarkably, on aesthetic generation, NTRK surpasses the reward of the best baseline at 500 NFEs using only 25 NFEs, a 20$\times$ reduction in compute.

</details>


### [131] [Volterra Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18071)
*Yusen Jia,Bingyan Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Volterra生成模型，一种基于分数核路径依赖噪声的连续时间分数扩散模型，通过有限维马尔可夫提升处理非马尔可夫动力学，并在MNIST和CIFAR-10上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分数扩散模型使用布朗噪声（马尔可夫、无记忆），缺乏路径依赖的噪声注入能力。本文旨在引入分数核驱动的路径依赖噪声，扩展扩散模型框架，同时解决非马尔可夫与非半鞅动力学带来的计算与学习挑战。

Method: 1) 前向过程使用分数核注入路径依赖噪声；2) 通过高斯求积（所有区域）和混合有限差指数逼近（光滑区域）构造有限维马尔可夫提升；3) 推导增广线性高斯前向过程，利用残差状态和解析辅助高斯分数保持学习维度与数据维度一致；4) 针对共享布朗因子和有符号光滑区域权重导致的协方差与逆向退化解，提出稳定条件化以及高斯桥重建采样器。

Result: 在MNIST上，使用小马尔可夫提升的持久分数扰动改进了分数生成性能；在CIFAR-10上，该方法提供了有前景的扩展；桥采样器为大提升提供了稳定性机制。

Conclusion: 分数核路径依赖噪声是扩散模型的一个有前景的扩展方向，有限维马尔可夫提升使得非马尔可夫分数扩散在计算上可行，而桥采样器有效缓解了大提升下的数值不稳定性。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models typically use Brownian perturbations, which provide tractable reverse-time dynamics but impose memoryless noising. We introduce Volterra generative models, a continuous-time score-based framework whose forward process injects path-dependent noise through fractional kernels. To handle the non-Markovian and non-semimartingale dynamics, we construct finite-dimensional Markovian lifts using Gaussian quadrature in both regimes and a hybrid finite-difference exponential approximation in the smooth regime. We prove squared error bounds, derive an augmented linear-Gaussian forward process, and show that the learning can remain data-dimensional by considering residual states and analytic auxiliary Gaussian scores. We also identify covariance and reverse-time degeneracies caused by shared Brownian factors and signed smooth-regime weights. The degeneracy motivates stabilized conditioning and, for stiff larger lifts, a Gaussian-bridge reconstruction sampler. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that persistent fractional perturbations with small Markovian lifts can improve score-based generation on MNIST and provide a promising extension to natural images, while the bridge sampler provides a stability mechanism for larger lifts.

</details>


### [132] [Edge Flow: A Tractable and Predictive Continuous-Time Model for Gradient Descent at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18080)
*Pierre Marion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Edge Flow，一个由三个耦合常微分方程组成的连续时间模型，用于精确描述深度学习中梯度下降在稳定边缘（EoS）阶段的动力学行为，该模型通过中心、振荡方向和振荡幅度的分解，揭示了锐度自稳定的反馈机制，并仅需两次梯度计算和一次Hessian-向量积即可离散化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析工具（如梯度流和下降引理）无法适用于深度学习中的稳定边缘（EoS）机制，该机制下损失Hessian的最大特征值徘徊在稳定性阈值2/η附近。因此需要寻找一个有效的连续时间模型来捕捉EoS下的梯度下降动力学。

Method: 提出Edge Flow模型，将EoS下的梯度下降动力学分解为三个耦合的常微分方程，分别对应中心（沿对称化损失的修正梯度流）、振荡方向（通过Rayleigh商动力学追踪Hessian主特征向量）和振荡幅度（根据锐度是否超过阈值2/η呈指数增长或衰减）。锐度稳定通过自稳定反馈环路实现。离散化仅需两次梯度计算和一次Hessian-向量积。

Result: 实验表明，Edge Flow在追踪梯度下降动力学方面至少与先前提出的连续时间EoS模型相当，并且能够进一步解析EoS起始阶段的锐度振荡现象。该模型为理解和缓解EoS机制下的不稳定性提供了理论框架。

Conclusion: Edge Flow是一个忠实、可预测且易于计算的EoS动力学模型，其自稳定反馈机制为理解深度学习在EoS阶段的训练行为提供了新视角，并为设计更稳定的优化算法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Gradient descent in deep learning may operate at the edge of stability (EoS), a regime in which the largest eigenvalue of the loss Hessian hovers near the stability threshold $2/η$, where $η$ is the learning rate. Classical analysis tools such as gradient flow and the descent lemma do not apply here, motivating the search for a continuous-time model valid at EoS. We propose Edge Flow, a system of three coupled ordinary differential equations that provides a tractable, faithful, and predictive model of gradient descent dynamics at EoS. Edge Flow decomposes the dynamics into a center, an oscillation direction, and an oscillation magnitude. The center follows a modified gradient flow on a symmetrized loss; the direction tracks a top eigenvector of the Hessian via Rayleigh quotient dynamics; and the magnitude grows or decays exponentially depending on whether the sharpness exceeds or falls below the threshold $2/η$. Crucially, sharpness stabilization emerges from the coupled dynamics via a self-stabilization feedback loop. Discretizing Edge Flow only requires two gradient evaluations and one Hessian--vector product at each iteration. We demonstrate empirically that Edge Flow tracks the dynamics of gradient descent at least as faithfully as previously proposed continuous-time EoS models, while in addition resolving the oscillation of the sharpness at the onset of EoS, and that it provides a principled framework for understanding and mitigating instabilities in this regime.

</details>


### [133] [From Reasoning Traces to Reusable Modules: Understanding Compositional Generalization in Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18089)
*Lingjing Kong,Xin Liu,Guangyi Chen,Martin Q. Ma,Xiangchen Song,Yuekai Sun,Mikhail Yurochkin,Taylor W. Killian,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Kun Zhang,Eric P. Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个形式化框架，将后训练流水线（SFT+RL）的成功归因于组合泛化。理论证明SFT提供可复用的原子模块材料，RL从组合轨迹中分解出这些模块并实现组合泛化。实验验证了RL能从SFT提供的复合轨迹中提取原子模块并重组解决新配置，且训练复合轨迹优于孤立原子模块。最终提出有效协议：SFT通过组合轨迹覆盖所有原子模块，RL聚焦SFT未覆盖的新组合以驱动探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释监督微调(SFT)与强化学习(RL)结合的后训练流水线为何能有效将大语言模型转化为强大的推理模型，揭示其成功背后的机制原理。

Method: 通过层次化潜在选择模型形式化组合泛化，理论分析SFT和RL的非对称互补角色，并设计受控实验验证理论。

Result: RL能从SFT提供的复合轨迹中提取原子模块并重组解决新配置；训练复合轨迹比训练孤立原子模块泛化更强；SFT覆盖所有原子模块，RL聚焦新组合是有效协议。

Conclusion: SFT和RL在后训练中扮演非对称互补角色：SFT提供原子模块材料，RL实现组合泛化。最佳策略是SFT通过组合轨迹确保原子模块覆盖，RL探索SFT未覆盖的新组合。

Abstract: Post-training pipelines that combine supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with reinforcement learning (RL) have emerged as the key recipe for transforming large language models (LLMs) into robust reasoners. We argue that this combined success is driven by compositional generalization, which we formalize through a hierarchical latent selection model. In this framework, reasoning traces are generated by a cascade of discrete latent selection variables corresponding to reusable atomic modules, including both skills (local operations) and routing mechanisms (how intermediate information is selected, reused, and composed). Within this model, we theoretically show that SFT and RL play asymmetric, complementary roles: SFT supplies the raw module materials in compositional traces, and RL decomposes those traces to identify the latent atomic modules and enable compositional generalization. We design controlled experiments to validate this theory. Our results demonstrate that RL can extract atomic modules from compound traces supplied by SFT and recombine them to solve new configurations. Moreover, we find that training on compound traces yields stronger generalization than training on isolated atomic modules. Finally, we investigate the relationship between SFT and RL data and identify an effective protocol in which SFT ensures coverage of all atomic modules through compositional traces, while RL focuses on novel compositions outside the SFT support to drive exploration.

</details>


### [134] [S4oP: Operator-level Pruning of Structured State Space Models for Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18096)
*Marco Deano,Filippo Ziche,Nicola Bombieri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了一种针对S4和S4D结构状态空间模型（SSMs）的增量式、算子级剪枝方法，通过交错结构掩码与微调来逐步剪枝模型算子，在保持预测性能的同时显著降低推理成本，实验表明剪枝多达70%的算子仍能在大多数情况下保持原有模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管S4和S4D等结构化状态空间模型在捕捉序列数据长距离依赖方面表现优异，但它们在时间和资源受限场景下的部署仍面临计算和内存需求过高的挑战，现有研究尚无系统性地探索结构化算子剪枝方法。

Method: 提出增量式、算子级剪枝方法，通过交错进行结构化掩码与微调来逐步剪枝模型算子，同时联合监控准确率和推理延迟，并在统一的训练和评估框架中实现，以系统探索效率-准确率之间的权衡。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，剪枝高达70%的模型算子在大多数情况下仍能保持原始模型的性能，同时显著降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: 结构化算子剪枝是提升SSMs效率的有效且此前未被探索的策略，有助于在资源受限的实际场景中部署这类模型。

Abstract: Structured State Space Models (SSMs), including the S4 and S4D architectures, have recently emerged as powerful alternatives to attention-based models for capturing long-range dependencies in sequential data. Despite their strong empirical performance, deploying these models in time- and resource-constrained settings remains challenging due to their computational and memory demands. In this paper, we propose a novel incremental, operator-level pruning approach for S4- and S4D-based models that significantly reduces inference cost while preserving predictive performance. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to systematically investigate structured operator pruning for SSMs. Our method progressively prunes model operators by interleaving structured masking with fine-tuning, while jointly monitoring accuracy and inference latency. We implement this approach within a unified training and evaluation framework that enables systematic exploration of efficiency-accuracy trade-offs. Experiments across multiple benchmark datasets show that pruning up to 70% of the model operators preserves the performance of the original models in most cases, while substantially reducing inference latency. These results demonstrate that structured operator pruning is an effective and previously unexplored strategy for improving the efficiency of SSMs and facilitate their deployment in practical, resource-constrained scenarios.

</details>


### [135] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Minimum Zero-Forcing Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18106)
*Steve Halley,Maurício Gruppi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习（SD-ZFS）的框架来解决图上的最小零强制集问题，该问题是NP难的。实验表明该框架在多个图数据集上优于贪心启发式方法，且具有良好的泛化、扩展和迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小零强制集问题在网络科学、网络控制和逻辑电路设计等多个领域有重要应用，但已被证明是NP难问题，现有方法难以高效求解，因此需要更优的求解框架。

Method: 基于强化学习框架SD-ZFS，该方法改编自S2V-DQN架构，通过将零强制集问题建模为序列决策问题，利用图神经网络表示图状态，并采用深度Q网络进行策略学习。

Result: 在多种结构不同的图数据集上，SD-ZFS框架的表现优于贪心启发式方法，与最优解相比也具有竞争力；模型展现出良好的泛化能力、可扩展性和跨网络类型的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 证明了强化学习框架能够有效求解最小零强制集问题，并揭示了网络结构对该问题求解的影响，为图上的NP难组合优化问题提供了新的机器学习求解思路。

Abstract: This paper explores the problem of finding the minimum zero-forcing set on undirected graphs and proposes an adapted machine-learning framework to solve the problem. The minimum zero-forcing set problem is a graph coloring problem where the color of an initial set of nodes propagates throughout a network. The set of nodes is zero-forcing if it forces all uncolored nodes to change color under the constraint of the color-change rule. There are several applications to this problem across different domains such as network science, network control, and designing logical circuits. Finding the minimum zero-forcing set is shown to be NP-hard. We propose a reinforcement learning framework, SD-ZFS, that adapts the S2V-DQN architecture to the ZFS problem. We train several models on this adapted framework and analyze the performance across graph datasets that have varying structures. We evaluate how the models trained on the framework generalize, scale, and transfer to different network types. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework when compared against the optimal solution and greedy heuristic. We provide further insight into how the ZFS problem can be solved through machine-learning and the influence of network structure on the problem.

</details>


### [136] [Learning Fair Pareto-Optimal Policies in Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18111)
*Umer Siddique,Peilang Li,Yongcan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多策略多目标强化学习中的公平优化问题，提出将广义基尼福利函数(GGF)等凹分段线性福利函数融入多策略学习框架，通过非平稳策略、状态增强和随机策略三种算法，学习一组兼顾帕累托最优性和公平性的策略集合，实验证明该方法能适应不同用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单策略MORL方法虽能用福利函数（如GGF）为固定用户偏好学习公平策略，但无法提供应对动态或未知用户偏好所需的多样化策略集合。

Method: (1) 理论证明凹分段线性福利函数的公平策略存在于凸覆盖集(CCS)中；(2) 提出非平稳策略（结合累积奖励历史）和随机策略可动态适应历史不公平性；(3) 提出三种算法：GGF+多策略MOQL、状态增强多策略MOQL（学习非平稳策略）、及其扩展用于学习随机策略。

Result: 在多个领域上的实验表明，所提方法能够学习一组公平策略，适应不同用户偏好，优于现有SOTA MORL基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将公平性福利函数引入多策略MORL框架，并结合非平稳与随机策略设计，可有效解决多目标决策中兼顾最优性与公平性的挑战。

Abstract: Fairness is an important aspect of decision-making in multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL), where policies must ensure both optimality and equity across multiple, potentially conflicting objectives. While single-policy MORL methods can learn fair policies for fixed user preferences using welfare functions such as the generalized Gini welfare function (GGF), they fail to provide the diverse set of policies necessary for dynamic or unknown user preferences. To address this limitation, we formalize the fair optimization problem in multi-policy MORL, where the goal is to learn a set of Pareto-optimal policies that ensure fairness across all possible user preferences. Our key technical contributions are threefold: (1) We show that for concave, piecewise-linear welfare functions (e.g., GGF), fair policies remain in the convex coverage set (CCS), which is an approximated Pareto front for linear scalarization. (2) We demonstrate that non-stationary policies, augmented with accrued reward histories, and stochastic policies improve fairness by dynamically adapting to historical inequities. (3) We propose three novel algorithms, which include integrating GGF with multi-policy multi-objective Q-Learning (MOQL), state-augmented multi-policy MOQL for learning non-statoinary policies, and its novel extension for learning stochastic policies. We evaluate our algorithms across various domains and compare our methods against the state-of-the-art MORL baselines. The empirical results show that our methods learn a set of fair policies that accommodate different user preferences.

</details>


### [137] [Ternary Mamba: Grouped Quantization-Aware Training of W1.58A16 State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18114)
*Ramprasath Ganesaraja,Sahil Dilip Panse,Swathika N*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用分组量化感知训练(QAT)结合知识蒸馏(KD)，从预训练的FP16 Mamba-2 1.3B检查点出发，仅用102M tokens（4 GPU小时）即可将模型压缩至3.61倍（744 MB），达到48.1%零样本准确率，接近Bi-Mamba的48.4%，相比从头训练（150B tokens）降低了1000倍的边际token预算。同时发现了"零比率崩溃"这一新现象，并指出Transformer的后处理校正策略因状态空间模型的循环结构导致的误差累积而失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态空间模型（如Mamba-2）虽具有线性时间推理能力，但其内存占用仍然过大，限制了在边缘设备上的部署。此前三元SSM工作（Slender-Mamba）需要从头训练150B tokens，训练成本极高。本文旨在探索是否可以利用预训练检查点进行数据高效的压缩，大幅降低训练成本。

Method: 采用分组量化感知训练（Grouped QAT），结合来自冻结的FP16教师模型的知识蒸馏（KD），从预训练的Mamba-2 1.3B检查点出发进行三元量化压缩。仅使用102M tokens（4 GPU小时，单张H100）完成训练。

Result: 将Mamba-2 1.3B模型从2,687 MB压缩至744 MB（3.61倍压缩），在7个任务的零样本准确率平均为48.1%，接近Bi-Mamba的48.4%（置信区间内相差+/-0.9pp）。

Conclusion: 三元SSM不需要昂贵的从头训练：从预训练检查点出发的QAT+KD是一种数据高效的替代方案。同时发现了"零比率崩溃"这一新现象，并指出后处理校正策略对SSM无效是因为误差通过循环结构累积。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) such as Mamba-2 offer linear-time inference but their memory footprint limits edge deployment. Prior ternary SSM work (Slender-Mamba) trains from scratch on 150B tokens; we show a pretrained checkpoint suffices, reducing the marginal token budget by 1,000x. Using grouped quantization-aware training (QAT) with knowledge distillation from a frozen FP16 teacher, we compress Mamba-2 1.3B to 3.61x (2,687 to 744 MB) and achieve 48.1% zero-shot accuracy (7-task average) in just 102M tokens (4 GPU-hours, single H100) -- approaching Bi-Mamba's 48.4% (within +/-0.9pp CI). This QAT-from-pretrained setting reveals zero-ratio collapse, a novel instability caused by learnable quantization scales that does not arise in from-scratch training. We further show that post-hoc correction strategies effective for Transformers fail for SSMs due to error accumulation through the recurrence. These results demonstrate that ternary SSMs do not require expensive from-scratch training: QAT from pretrained checkpoints with KD is a data-efficient alternative.

</details>


### [138] [Embedded Machine Learning for Microcontroller-Class Edge Devices: Data, Feature, Evaluation, and Deployment Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18122)
*Mostafa Darvishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地介绍了面向微控制器平台的嵌入式机器学习工作流，涵盖数据采样、特征提取、模型/运行时协同设计及流式部署等工程决策，并通过惯性运动识别和关键词唤醒两个案例验证了设计规则的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式机器学习需在资源受限设备上完成推理，但通用机器学习教程常忽略采样缓冲、特征降维、类别不平衡验证、模型与运行时协同设计及流式部署等关键工程决策，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用系统化视角，以两类代表性信号（惯性运动识别和关键词唤醒）贯穿全文，详述从数据获取到部署的完整工作流，重点阐述工程决策要点。惯性运动识别中提取加速度计原始样本的均方根和频谱特征；关键词唤醒中经抗混叠滤波后提取梅尔频率倒谱系数，由紧凑型一维卷积网络处理。

Result: 提出了面向微控制器的嵌入式机器学习工作流，包括数据采样与缓冲策略、特征提取作为降维方法、类别不平衡下的验证方法、模型与运行时的协同设计以及流式部署方案。

Conclusion: 归纳了鲁棒端侧推理的实践设计规则，涵盖数据整理、量化、阈值设定、调度与现场监控等方面。

Abstract: Embedded machine learning moves inference from cloud services to resource-constrained devices that must acquire data, preprocess signals, run a model, and act within tight limits on memory, energy, and latency. This paper presents a systems-oriented synthesis of an embedded machine-learning workflow for microcontroller-class platforms. The emphasis is placed on engineering decisions that are often hidden in generic machine-learning introductions: sampling and buffering, feature extraction as dimensionality reduction, validation under class imbalance, model/runtime co-design, and streaming deployment. Two representative signal families are used throughout the paper. The first is inertial motion recognition, where a two-second, three-axis accelerometer window is transformed from raw samples into root-mean-square and spectral features before classification. The second is keyword spotting, where audio is sampled, anti-aliased, transformed into mel-frequency cepstral coefficients, and processed by a compact one-dimensional convolutional network. The paper concludes with practical design rules for robust on-device inference, including data curation, quantization, thresholding, scheduling, and field monitoring.

</details>


### [139] [Kolmogorov Regression for Robust Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18186)
*Lekan Molu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于后向Kolmogorov方程的有限维扩散策略，通过将扩散策略提升到Cameron-Martin空间并引入确定性边界值PDE问题，解决了离散化伪影导致的时间漂移问题。在PushT操控基准和CONWIP制造线控制任务上，分别实现了17%的奖励提升和28.4%的RMSE降低，并通过Hamilton-Jacobi可达性理论将死锁事件减少96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维扩散策略因离散化伪影导致时间漂移，影响长时域部署性能，亟需一种能保证轨迹正则性和收敛性的方法。

Method: 引入后向Kolmogorov方程将扩散策略提升到Cameron-Martin空间；利用高斯测度理论从有色噪声分布实现扩散噪声协方差算子；提出精度加权的Cameron-Martin损失训练扩散模型；引入Kolmogorov残差作为推理时的PDE诊断工具；结合Hamilton-Jacobi可达性理论验证调度策略的安全性。

Result: PushT基准：最大回合奖励提升17%（0.95 vs 0.78），步间漂移减少67.6%；制造线控制：RMSE降低28.4%，饥饿事件召回率达1.0，瓶颈识别Precision@1=1.0，信噪比提升13倍；死锁事件减少96%。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过PDE框架替代随机得分匹配，提供了收敛性保证、改进的轨迹正则性和无奖励信号的确定性故障检测能力，在机器人操控和制造控制领域均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Finite-dimensional (FD) diffusion policies exhibit temporal drift owing to discretization artifacts that degrade long-horizon performance (when deployed on physical systems). We introduce a backward Kolmogorov equation that lifts diffusion policies to a Cameron-Martin space -- a subset of the Hilbert space. Essentially, replacing stochastic score matching with a deterministic boundary-value PDE problem. Our core innovation thrives on Gaussian measure theory whereupon the diffusion noise covariance operator is realized from a colored noise distribution which prescribes a notion of regularity on samples from the model at inference time. We train the diffusion model with a derived precision-weighted Cameron- Martin loss and a Kolmogorov residual is introduced as a PDE diagnostic during inference. These substitutions yield (i) convergence guarantees where the bound's constants depend on the effective rank of the kernel rather than action dimension, (ii) improved trajectory regularity via spectral weighting, and (iii) a deterministic failure detector without reward signals. Validation across two application domains demonstrates substantial improvements: on the PushT manipulation benchmark, the Cameron-Martin loss achieves a 17% improvement in maximum episode reward (0.95 vs. 0.78 for MSE) and 67.6% reduction in inter-step drifts during inference via the introduced residual magnitude. Similarly, on a 6-station manufacturing line with constant work-in-process (CONWIP) flow control, we achieve 28.4% lower RMSE than classical LSTM baselines; a high starvation-event recall (1.0 in test cycles), and effective bottleneck identification (Precision@1 = 1.0 in test set, 13x signal-to-noise ratio). We then certify the dispatch policies with Hamilton-Jacobi reachability theory which reduces deadlock events by 96% compared to uncontrolled dispatch over 100 simulated runs (351 events prevented).

</details>


### [140] [Looped World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18208)
*Hongyuan Adam Lu,Z. L. Victor Wei,Qun Zhang,Jinrui Zeng,Bowen Cao,Lingwei Meng,Mocheng Li,Zezhong Wang,Haonan Yin,Naifu Xue,Minyu Chen,Cenyuan Zhang,Zefan Zhang,Hao Wei,Jiawei Zhou,Haoran Xu,Hao Yang,Ronglai Zuo,Tongda Xu,Yonghao Li,Jian Chen,Hebin Wang,Zeyu Gao,Yang Li,Wei Zhao,Qimin Zhong,Siqi Liu,Yumeng Zhang,Leyan Cui,Zhangyu Wang,Wai Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopWM 提出循环世界模型架构，通过参数共享的 Transformer 块迭代细化潜状态，实现高达 100 倍参数效率，并将迭代潜深度确立为世界模拟的新缩放轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型面临根本性矛盾：长程保真模拟需要深度计算，但深层模型部署成本高且易产生累积误差。

Method: 提出首个循环架构的世界模型 LoopWM，使用参数共享的 Transformer 块对潜环境状态进行迭代细化，实现自适应计算深度。

Result: 相比传统方法实现高达 100 倍的参数效率，且计算深度能自动匹配每个预测步骤的复杂度。

Conclusion: 迭代潜深度可作为独立于模型规模和训练数据的新缩放轴，推动世界模拟领域发展。

Abstract: Current world models face a fundamental tension: faithful long-horizon simulation demands deep computation, but deeper models are expensive to deploy and prone to compounding errors. We resolve this by introducing Looped World Models (LoopWM), which are the first looped architectures for world modelling. Our method iteratively refines latent environment states through a parameter-shared transformer block. This yield up to 100x parameter efficiency over conventional approaches with adaptive computation that automatically scales depth to match the complexity of each prediction step. Orthogonal to scaling model size and training data, LoopWM establishes iterative latent depth as a new scaling axis for world simulation, which might significantly push the community forward.

</details>


### [141] [Rethinking Dataset Distillation for Classification: Do Distilled Sets Outperform Coresets?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18209)
*Trisha Mittal,Akshay Mehra,Joshua Kimball*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模标准化实验，系统评估了数据集蒸馏（DD）方法与核心集选择（CS）方法的性能对比。结果表明，当前最先进的DD方法在大规模数据集上并不优于甚至不如CS方法，且构建成本更高，而CS在数据分布覆盖和计算效率方面具有明显优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DD方法的评估协议不一致（从标准ERM到单/多教师监督），难以区分蒸馏数据的真实效果与评估方式的影响；且许多方法声称DD优于数据剪枝（核心集选择），但缺乏公平、大规模的验证。

Method: 在ImageNet-1K、ImageNet100、ImageNette上，使用三种广泛采用的训练协议，对七种SOTA DD方法与三种CS策略进行标准化基准测试；除准确率外，还评估了数据集的代表性、多样性和质量。

Result: 部分DD方法甚至不如随机子集；SOTA DD方法在大规模数据集上与核心集相当或更差，且构建成本显著更高；核心集在原始数据分布覆盖上始终优于蒸馏集。

Conclusion: 当前DD方法的实际优势有限，核心集仍是一个更具竞争力的计算高效替代方案。

Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) has emerged as a prominent approach in data centric machine learning, aiming to synthesize compact training sets for efficient training by compressing the information in large datasets into a small number of synthetic samples. However, DD methods are often evaluated under inconsistent evaluation protocols, ranging from standard ERM to single/multi-teacher supervision, making it difficult to isolate the effectiveness of distilled data from evaluation. Moreover, many prior methods claim that DD outperforms data pruning approaches such as coreset selection (CS), based on the assumption that restricting condensed datasets to subsets of real samples fundamentally limits their expressiveness. In this work, we critically evaluate DD methods through large-scale experiments using standardized datasets and evaluation protocols to assess their intrinsic effectiveness. We benchmark seven state-of-the-art (SOTA) DD methods on ImageNet-1K, ImageNet100, and ImageNette, using three widely adopted training protocols against three CS strategies. Our results show that while some DD methods fail to outperform even simple random subsets, the SOTA DD approaches are comparable to or worse than coresets on large-scale datasets and incur a substantially higher cost for construction. Beyond accuracy, we also evaluate the representativeness, diversity, and quality of condensed sets, and find that coresets consistently achieve better coverage of the original data distribution. These findings highlight the limited practical advantages of current DD methods and show that coresets remain competitive and are often a more computationally efficient alternative for data-centric learning.

</details>


### [142] [Sign-Rank, Index, and List Replicability: Connections and Separations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18236)
*Ari Blondal,Hamed Hatami,Pooya Hatami,Chavdar Lalov,Sivan Tretiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了概念类的符号秩下界问题，通过比较$\mathbb{Z}_2$-指数和列表可复现数这两种度量，证明了$\mathbb{Z}_2$-指数受列表可复现数的线性函数上界约束，进而解决了Frick等人提出的关于符号秩与$\mathbb{Z}_2$-指数分离的问题。此外，建立了列表可复现数与高度、最小星数等组合度量的上界关系，并证明了乘积概念类的列表可复现数满足可加性的组合结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号秩是学习理论中衡量二元概念类表达复杂度的核心概念，但已知下界方法极少且难以分析。近期出现的$\mathbb{Z}_2$-指数和列表可复现数两种方法提供了新的视角，本文旨在厘清二者之间的序关系，并深入探索更强的下界度量——列表可复现数的性质。

Method: 通过理论推导建立$\mathbb{Z}_2$-指数与列表可复现数之间的不等式关系；利用组合度量（高度和最小星数）对列表可复现数进行上界刻画；通过构造性证明得出乘积概念类的列表可复现数可加性定理。

Result: 证明了$\mathbb{Z}_2$-指数是列表可复现数的线性函数的上界，由此实现了符号秩与$\mathbb{Z}_2$-指数的强分离；建立了列表可复现数与高度、最小星数的上界关系；证明了乘积概念类的列表可复现数不超过两个类列表可复现数之和。

Conclusion: 列表可复现数是比$\mathbb{Z}_2$-指数更紧的符号秩下界度量，且具有良好的组合性质，为未来研究符号秩下界提供了更强大的理论工具。

Abstract: In learning theory, the sign rank of a binary concept class captures the smallest dimension in which it can be represented by points and halfspaces. Despite tremendous interest, lower bounds on sign rank are notoriously difficult to come by. Two recent approaches to the problem establish lower bounds on sign rank by measures that are easier to analyze: the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index and the list replicability number.
  We order these measures, showing that the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index is upper-bounded by a linear function of the list replicability number. As a main consequence, we obtain a strong separation between sign rank and $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index, thereby resolving a question of Frick, Hosseini, and Vasileuski.
  This motivates a thorough study of list replicability, the stronger of the two lower-bounding measures. We establish upper bounds on the list replicability number by two combinatorial measures: height and minimum star number. We also prove a fundamental composition result, showing that the product of two concept classes has list replicability number bounded by the sum of the list replicability numbers of the two classes.

</details>

### [143] [Tensor network compression using fluid dynamics as a testbed: Analytical foundations in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17064)
*Matthew D. Horner,Callum W. Duncan,Oliver T. Brown,Stephen M. de Bruyn Kops,Muralikrishnan Gopalakrishnan Meena*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量网络（矩阵乘积态/张量列）的数据压缩方法，适用于高性能计算产生的极端规模数据，特别是流体动力学数据。该方法具有客观、鲁棒、可调谐的特点，在一维压缩中展示了无损和有损压缩能力，并支持高维扩展。压缩后的数据可直接进行周期性卷积计算，速度可比快速傅里叶变换快数个数量级，且与量子计算范式兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算产生的极端规模数据集需要采样或压缩才能充分利用，但现有压缩技术通常依赖数据的稀疏性、均匀性或先验知识，而流体动力学数据不具备这些特征。因此需要一种客观、鲁棒、可调谐的通用压缩技术。

Method: 基于张量网络（矩阵乘积态/张量列）的压缩方法：通过调节张量网络的键维度（bond dimension）实现无损或可控有损压缩；存储规模与键维度呈线性关系；支持通过张量网络周期性卷积在压缩形式上直接进行计算。

Result: 对于随机傅里叶级数，在足够高的键维度下实现无损压缩；低键维度的有损压缩误差可控制在流体仿真相对误差范围内；在Burger方程时间演化测试中表现优异；张量网络周期性卷积相比快速傅里叶变换可加速数个数量级。

Conclusion: 张量网络方法为流体动力学等不具备稀疏性或均匀性特征的数据提供了有效的压缩方案，兼具计算加速能力，且可直接迁移至量子计算范式，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: High performance computers produce extreme-scale data sets that require sampling or compression if they are to be used to their full potential. Existing data compression techniques typically exploit features such as sparsity in the data, homogeneity in the data, or {\it a priori} knowledge of what subsets of data are of most interest. Fluid dynamics data in general do not exhibit these features and so are attractive test beds for generic compression techniques that are objective, robust, and tuneable with respect to information lost due to compression. Presented here is a method based on tensor networks, specifically matrix product states or tensor trains, that meets these requirements. The method is demonstrated for compression in one-dimension and is extensible to higher dimensionality. Lossless compression is demonstrated for random Fourier series for sufficiently high bond dimension of the tensor network, with the memory required to store the tensor network scaling directly proportional to the bond dimension. The lossy compression exhibited at lower bond dimension can be well within the relative error of many fluid simulations. The compression algorithm is tested for the time evolution of Burger's equation with excellent results. We additionally demonstrate the capability to perform computations in the compressed form through a tensor network periodic convolution that can be orders of magnitude faster than using fast Fourier transforms and the convolution theorem. In addition to being an attractive method for working with data sets generated by existing computers, the tensor network methods utilised are directly translatable to the emerging paradigm of quantum computing.

</details>


### [144] [Constitutive modelling of magneto-active polymers at finite strains: A survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17576)
*Abhishek Ghosh,Chennakesava Kadapa,Mokarram Hossain*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了磁活性聚合物（MAPs）在有限应变下的本构建模方法，涵盖从早期半经验模型到热力学一致的非线性连续介质理论的发展历程，包括各向同性/各向异性磁弹性模型、不变量与谱表示法、变分与多凸框架、微结构启发模型、分散链描述以及磁粘弹性理论，并指出了当前面临的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAPs因其磁场可调的力学性能（如场致硬化、磁致伸缩、各向异性和率相关响应）在传感器、执行器、自适应结构、振动控制和软体机器人等领域具有重要应用前景，系统梳理其本构建模方法的发展脉络与现状对于该领域的进一步发展至关重要。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，按照历史发展脉络和理论分类，系统梳理了MAPs在有限应变下的各类本构建模方法，包括：早期半经验模型、热力学一致的非线性连续介质理论、各向同性与各向异性磁弹性模型、不变量与谱表示法、变分与多凸框架、微结构启发模型、分散链描述以及磁粘弹性理论，并讨论了主要本构变量、能量函数、耦合机制和物理假设。

Result: MAPs本构建模已发展为一类能够描述实验观察到的多种行为的非线性、各向异性且含耗散的理论框架体系；当前仍面临热-磁-力学耦合、微结构敏感效应、参数识别、模型验证以及计算机鲁棒高效实现等重要挑战。

Conclusion: MAPs本构建模已取得显著进展，形成了涵盖非线性、各向异性和耗散特性的理论家族，但在多场耦合、微结构效应、参数识别、模型验证和计算实现方面仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Magneto-active polymers (MAPs) are field-responsive soft composites whose mechanical behaviour can be actively modified by external magnetic fields. Their ability to exhibit field-induced stiffening, magnetostriction, anisotropy and rate-dependent response makes them attractive for sensors, actuators, adaptive structures, vibration-control devices and soft robotic systems. This article presents a structured survey of constitutive modelling approaches for MAPs at finite strains. The review traces the development from early semi-empirical descriptions to thermodynamically consistent nonlinear continuum theories, with particular attention to isotropic and anisotropic magnetoelastic constitutive models, invariant-based and spectral representations, variational and polyconvex frameworks, microstructurally motivated models, dispersed-chain descriptions and magneto-viscoelastic theories. The principal constitutive variables, energy functions, coupling mechanisms and physical assumptions underlying the main models are discussed. The survey shows that constitutive modelling of MAPs has developed into a broad family of nonlinear, anisotropic and dissipative frameworks capable of describing versatile behaviours observed experimentally. Important challenges remain in resolving thermo-magneto-mechanical coupling and microstructure-sensitive effects, identification of parameters, validation of models as well as robust and efficient implementation on computers.

</details>


### [145] [Latent Residual-Closure Fourier Neural Operator for Robust Multi-Field Solving in Particle-in-Cell Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17733)
*Jianhua Lyu,Linlin Zhong*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种潜变量残差闭合傅里叶神经算子（LRC-FNO），通过两级残差闭合框架（源压缩-潜变量闭合精炼-粗网格求解-残差闭合恢复）实现对PIC模拟中多物理场的鲁棒代理求解，在1D/2D三个基准测试中表现出高精度和物理一致性，并可作为迭代求解器的优质初始猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIC仿真中反复在稠密网格上进行场求解效率低下，亟需一种高效的代理场求解器来加速计算，同时保持物理一致性。

Method: 将PIC场求解形式化为两级残差闭合问题：自编码器压缩粒子沉积源场，潜变量闭合精炼器恢复压缩中丢失的残差结构，粗网格FNO求解器捕获主导场响应，残差闭合FNO恢复全分辨率修正。

Result: 在SOL聚变等离子体模型中，单步预测自洽势相对L2误差0.0447，磁矢势0.0251；作为神经初始猜测配合20次迭代修正，可在接近两倍训练时域内保持电荷/电流密度结构。

Conclusion: LRC-FNO既可作快速代理场求解器，也可为迭代PIC场求解器提供高质量初始化策略。

Abstract: Particle-in-cell (PIC) simulations are widely used for kinetic plasma modeling in energy applications, but their efficiency is often limited by repeated field solves on dense meshes. This work proposes a Latent Residual-Closure Fourier Neural Operator (LRC-FNO) for robust surrogate multi-field solving in PIC simulations. Rather than treating field prediction as a purely data-driven regression task, LRC-FNO formulates PIC field solving as a two-level residual-closure problem involving source compression and source-to-field operator mapping. An autoencoder extracts compact representations of particle-deposited source fields, while a Latent Closure Refiner recovers unresolved residual structures lost during compression. A Coarse-FNO Solver captures the dominant field response, and a Residual-Closure FNO restores full-resolution corrections. The method is tested on three benchmarks with increasing complexity: 1D linear Landau damping (LLD), 2D two-stream instability (TSI), and a 2D scrape-off layer (SOL) fusion plasma model. In LLD and TSI, LRC-FNO better preserves charge-to-potential mapping, potential-mode evolution, residual charge structures, and particle-field energy exchange during closed-loop PIC integration. In the SOL case, LRC-FNO achieves relative L2 errors of 0.0447 for the self-consistent potential and 0.0251 for the magnetic vector potential in single-step prediction. More importantly, when used as a neural initial guess with 20 iterative corrections, LRC-FNO maintains strong physical consistency in extrapolated closed-loop simulations, preserving charge and current density structures over a time range close to twice the training horizon. These results demonstrate that LRC-FNO can serve as both a fast surrogate field solver and a high-quality initialization strategy for iterative PIC field solvers.

</details>


### [146] [High-Order Simulation of Particle-Laden Flows in Moving Domains Using Coupled ALE and Sliding Mesh Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17969)
*Anna Schwarz,Patrick Kopper*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高保真欧拉-拉格朗日框架，耦合高阶间断伽辽金谱元法与拉格朗日点粒子追踪，结合任意拉格朗日-欧拉法和滑移网格两种网格运动策略，用于模拟运动几何中可压缩粒子负载流，并在压缩机转子应用中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中运动几何内的可压缩粒子负载流涉及复杂、非线性、多尺度的湍流结构相互作用，数值求解需要精细的算法处理以准确预测粒子轨迹，现有方法在运动网格与粒子追踪的紧耦合方面存在不足。

Method: 采用高阶间断伽辽金谱元法求解连续相，结合拉格朗日点粒子追踪；集成任意拉格朗日-欧拉法（一般网格变形，如粒子诱导表面变形）和滑移网格法（刚性旋转/平移）；通过径向基函数变形实现ALE与时间演化的紧耦合；确保网格运动算法与离散相的时间高阶精度耦合，特别解决了粒子穿越非一致滑移网格界面时的时空精度问题。

Result: 通过多个基准测试严格验证算法，并应用于两个具有挑战性的压缩机转子问题：固体颗粒侵蚀模拟，以及上游圆柱尾流发生器场景。

Conclusion: 该框架能够高精度处理运动几何中粒子负载流问题，尤其解决了滑移网格下粒子跨界面追踪的时空精度难题，为压缩机等工程应用提供了可靠的数值工具。

Abstract: In practical applications, compressible particle-laden flows in moving geometries involve complex, non-linear, and multi-scale inter-actions with turbulent structures. Resolving these dynamics numerically requires careful algorithmic treatment to accurately predict particle trajectories. This work presents a high-fidelity Euler-Lagrange framework that couples a high-order discontinuous Galerkin spectral element method for the continuous phase with a Lagrangian point-particle tracking scheme. To manage moving and deforming domains, the framework integrates two distinct mesh movement strategies: the arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian method for general mesh deformations such as time-resolved particle-induced surface deformations and its special interface case, the sliding mesh approach, uniquely suited for rigid rotational or translational movements. A primary focus is placed on tightly coupling the arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian formulation into the temporal evolution step by utilizing radial basis function morphing to capture the non-linear feedback loop between evolving surface topologies and the continuous phase. Concurrently, the framework ensures time- and high-order accurate coupling of the mesh movement algorithms with the dispersed phase. In particular, the proposed algorithm resolves the sliding mesh tracking problem by enforcing strict spatial and temporal accuracy as Lagrangian particles cross non-conforming grid interfaces between adjacent moving zones. The algorithms are rigorously validated against multiple benchmarks and subsequently applied to two challenging compressor rotor applications: the first focusing on solid-particle erosion, and the second featuring an upstream cylindrical wake generator.

</details>


### [147] [Singular Vector Finite Element Basis Functions for Tetrahedra in Complex Electromagnetic Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18140)
*Samuel T. Elkin,Ghazi Khan,Ebrahim Forati,Brandon W. Langley,Dogan Timucin,Reza Molavi,Thomas E. Roth*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对三维四面体单元开发了加性奇异基函数，用于精确模拟导体楔形结构等奇异特征附近的电磁场行为。与传统基函数相比，这些基函数能显著提升计算精度并大幅降低计算成本（例如在超导量子比特仿真中，从Ansys HFSS的21.27小时/6.23 TB内存/800核降至196秒/27.24 GB/16核），使得原本昂贵的仿真变得高效可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的电磁有限元方法在使用标准基函数时，难以精确表示导体楔形等奇异特征附近的场行为。虽然二维奇异基函数已有广泛研究，但三维四面体单元的奇异基函数开发尚不充分，限制了复杂三维电磁结构的高效仿真。

Method: 开发了适用于三维四面体的加性奇异基函数，这些基函数与标准矢量基函数共同使用（而非替换），通过针对四面体中每个节点和边的奇异基函数组合，使其能够适应多种不同的奇异特征。同时提供了高阶插值版本以提高精度。

Result: 与标准基函数相比，所提出的奇异基函数显著提升了计算精度。在超导量子比特仿真应用中，Ansys HFSS需要21.27小时、6.23 TB峰值内存和800个处理器，而使用本文奇异基函数仅需196秒、27.24 GB内存和16个处理器，即可获得可比结果。

Conclusion: 所开发的奇异基函数能够大幅降低电磁仿真成本，尤其适用于具有主导奇异行为的电磁几何结构（如超导量子比特）的设计优化。

Abstract: Electromagnetic finite element method (FEM) implementations using traditional basis functions struggle to accurately represent field behavior near singular features such as conducting wedges. To combat this, specialized singular basis functions have been introduced to directly model the singular fields in these regions, leading to substantially improved performance. While these efforts have been pursued extensively in 2D, few functions have been developed for 3D elements. In this work, we develop basis functions for this in tetrahedra. Unlike prior functions, these basis functions are additive, meaning they are included alongside the standard vector basis functions to achieve more robust performance. Further, these functions are designed to be adaptable to tetrahedra touching several unique singular features by using combinations of basis functions singular with respect to each node and edge in the element, making them applicable to highly complex geometries. Higher-order interpolatory versions of the basis functions for modeling singular behavior with greater accuracy are also provided. These basis functions lead to substantial improvements in accuracy relative to the standard basis functions, and allow otherwise expensive simulations to be performed at far lower costs. As an application example, we perform simulations to extract critical quantities for designing superconducting qubits that significantly depend on the behavior of singular fields. In Ansys HFSS, this took 21.27 hours and a peak memory usage of 6.23 TB with 800 processors available, while using our singular basis functions achieved comparable results in 196 seconds while using 27.24 GB of memory and only 16 processors. Due to these benefits, our singular basis functions could be applied to enable design optimization of electromagnetic geometries with dominantly singular behavior, such as superconducting qubits.

</details>
